What is printed below is each run's stdout and stderr as written, but for
lines dropped by one filter, named here so nothing is dropped silently:
the cage's own warnings, which every shell of every arm prints at every
start (MESA-EGL, the host portal, the Hyprland signature, layershell,
wl_egl_window, eglSwapBuffers); the plugin's two start-up notes, the
config shield's mode change and the bar entry's migration, which every
fresh install prints once; and cache.py's lines for files whose text is
the same in all four builds (Model.mjs and model/*.mjs but build.mjs),
whose verdict cannot name a build. The middle dot of a guide footer is
written \xb7. A "read" near a merge may be git's own open: the open watch
sees every open, and git re-reads files it checks before and after it
writes them (inferred, not traced); the verdicts rest on the marks and the
cache files.

SUMMARY (docs/QA-RESULTS.md "D-REL-14: call (d), settled (lane-settle-c5d,
2026-11-24)" holds the reading).
- Q6, root b (no pragma): 4 races, merge at the cold start's first read of
  Service.qml, C1 to C2 twice and C0 to C1 twice. All 4 left Service.qml's
  cache unit POISONED: its sourceChecksum the MD5 of the new file on disk
  (39edcc28.. for C2, 47d89e37.. for C1), its string table the old build's
  (C1's mark, or C0's none), sourceTimeStamp -1. Each mix ran again after
  two restarts: C1's Service.qml with C2's code-behinds, status throwing
  "TypeError: Property 'socketAttached' ... is not a function"; or C0's
  Service.qml with C1's model, build running 0.99.0 = onDisk, stale False,
  silent. Recovered, each twice: the cache folder removed and a restart;
  a later update (C3, or C2 after C0 to C1) and a restart. The cache is
  $XDG_CACHE_HOME/quickshell/qmlcache, one file per source named by the
  SHA-1 of its path (26 files on root b, 23 on root a).
- Q7, root a (the pragma, the host's root today): no unit is ever written
  for Service.qml, Guide.qml or BarWidget.qml (the load patch serves them
  through qs:), while the code-behinds and the model are cached, each unit
  saved 0.2-5 ms after its read. The sweep, 24 races (merge at the
  scanner's read of Service.qml, +615 to +665 ms, one worker and four):
  5 POISONED a code-behind and the mix survived two restarts
  (Service-player.js 4 times, status throwing "TypeError: Property
  'socketAttachedLive' ... is not a function"; Service-sources.js once,
  silent), each recovered by removing the cache folder; 3 loaded no
  service at the race (four workers: a code-behind unlinked when read),
  and 16 ran a mix or the old build at the race, each recovered by the
  restart. U: 4 more races with a later update for recovery: 2 POISONED
  (Service-rewind.js, Service-sources.js), both recovered by the C3 update
  and a restart.
- Q8: on root a the scanner reads Service.qml and the engine reads the
  code-behinds 658.7-707.1 ms later (four arms); on root b 4.7-15.5 ms
  later (three arms). At four workers git unlinked every changed file
  before any worker created one in 15 of 15 runs (Service.qml absent
  5.1-8.2 ms), and in 3 of 15 Service.qml closed before Service-player.js
  or Service-rewind.js was created or closed (0.1-1.1 ms, h4's shape). At
  one worker every code-behind closed before the old Service.qml was
  unlinked in 20 of 20 merges.
- h1 re-run with the faithful tree (every changed file before Service.qml
  in byte order from the new build): both roots ran C1's Service.qml with
  C2's code-behinds, guide and bar, status throwing the TypeError, the
  same type at every reload, rescan and enable; the restart recovered.
